({m_{\textsc{ii}}}-{m_{\textsc{i}}})^{{{j}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,.

(6.4)

With this, we can now formulate a simple model of the beam splitter.
Consider incoming radiation at the surface
\Sigma_{\textsc{in}}
(coming from a laser) of the form

F_{\textsc{in}}=\sqrt{2}K_{\textsc{in}}\wedge E_{\textsc{in}}e^{i\psi}\,,

(6.5)

where
E_{\textsc{in}}
is as in
Eq.

4.4
and the normalisation is such that the emitted radiation has unit energy density.

We then assume the fields at the output ports (facing the mirrors) to be

\displaystyle\text{on }\Sigma_{\textsc{i}}:

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}K_{\textsc{i}}\wedge E_{\textsc{i}}e^{i\psi}\,,

\displaystyle\text{on }\Sigma_{\textsc{ii}}:

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}K_{\textsc{ii}}\wedge E_{\textsc{ii}}e^{i\psi}\,.

(6.6)

The outgoing fields have only half the amplitude of the incoming field, in accordance with energy conservation.
This means that half the radiation passes through the beam splitter unchanged, while the other half is reflected along the normal
\tilde{\nu}
defined above.

Here,
K_{{\textsc{i}},{\textsc{ii}}}=\omega(\nu_{{\textsc{i}},{\textsc{ii}}}-\mathrm{d}t)
are the wave vectors normal to the respective surfaces, and the polarisation vectors are solutions of the respective constraint equations which satisfy the following relations
at the spatial origin

\displaystyle E_{\textsc{i}}

\displaystyle=E_{\textsc{in}}\,,

\displaystyle E_{\textsc{ii}}

\displaystyle=-R_{\text{BS}}E_{\textsc{in}}\,.

(6.7)

To obtain an expression for the amplitude everywhere on
\Sigma
, one must then construct a solution to the constraint equations with this prescribed behaviour at the spatial origin. The method of doing so is described in
Appendix

C
, but the precise details are irrelevant for the applications where.

These fields propagate towards the mirrors, are reflected there, and return to the beam splitter.
In the course of this propagation, the fields undergo changes as described by the equations (
5.17
)—(
5.20
).
Irrespective of the precise form of this transformation, the returning radiation (restricted to
\Sigma_{\textsc{i}}
and
\Sigma_{\textsc{ii}}
) can be written in the form

\displaystyle\text{on }\Sigma_{\textsc{i}}:

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\check{K}_{\textsc{i}}\wedge\check{E}_{\textsc{i}}e^{i\check{\psi}_{\textsc{i}}}\,,

\displaystyle\text{on }\Sigma_{\textsc{ii}}:

\displaystyle\hskip 10.00002pt\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\check{K}_{\textsc{ii}}\wedge\check{E}_{\textsc{ii}}e^{i\check{\psi}_{\textsc{ii}}}\,,

(6.8)

Finally, these fields pass through the beam splitter to reach the output port. To model this, we assume the radiation impinging at
\Sigma_{\textsc{i}}
to be deflected as above, while the radiation impinging at
\Sigma_{\textsc{ii}}
is inverted because of the
\pi
phase shift at a beam splitter.
This translates to the following formula for the output field at the spatial origin:

F_{\textsc{out}}=-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(R_{\text{BS}}\check{K}_{\textsc{i}})\wedge(R_{\text{BS}}\check{E}_{\textsc{i}})e^{i\check{\psi}_{\textsc{i}}}-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\check{K}_{\textsc{ii}}\wedge\check{E}_{\textsc{ii}}e^{i\check{\psi}_{\textsc{ii}}}\,.

(6.9)

7
Interferometer Output Signal

Having developed a description of light propagation, reflection at mirrors and at beam splitters, all in the presence of a plane gravitational wave, we can now compute the output of a simple Michelson interferometer as sketched in
Fig.

1
.

Ray I

One ray is first transmitted by the beam splitter, then reflects at the mirror surface
{\{{m_{\textsc{i}}}.x=\ell_{\textsc{i}}\}}
, and propagates back to the beam splitter where it is finally deflected towards the detector.

The radiation sent into the interferometer arm is precisely of the form (
4.12
)—(
4.14
), rescaled only by a factor
1/\sqrt{2}
in accordance with
Eq.

6.6
. Up to this global factor, the field returning to the beam splitter is thus obtained from
Eq.

5.16
by the substitution

\displaystyle m

\displaystyle\to m_{\textsc{i}}\,,

\displaystyle\ell

\displaystyle\to\ell_{\textsc{i}}\,,

\displaystyle k

\displaystyle\to k_{\textsc{i}}\,,

\displaystyle x^{E}

\displaystyle\to x^{E}_{\textsc{i}}\,,

\displaystyle x^{R}

\displaystyle\to x^{R}_{\textsc{i}}\,,

(7.1)

where
x^{E}_{\textsc{i}}
and
x^{R}_{\textsc{i}}
are defined as in
Eq.

5.15
, except for this same substitution:

\displaystyle\mathchoice{(x^{R}_{\textsc{i}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{R}_{\textsc{i}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{R}_{\textsc{i}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{R}_{\textsc{i}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-\ell_{\textsc{i}}\mathchoice{{\check{k}}^{{{\mu}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}}}}{{\check{k}}^{{{\mu}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}}}}{{\check{k}}^{{{\mu}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}}}}{{\check{k}}^{{{\mu}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}}}}/\omega\,,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{(x^{E}_{\textsc{i}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{E}_{\textsc{i}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{E}_{\textsc{i}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{E}_{\textsc{i}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle=(t-2\ell_{\textsc{i}},\mathchoice{x^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})\,.

(7.2)

According to
Eq.

6.9
, the corresponding radiation reaching the output port of the beam splitter is obtained by applying
R_{\text{BS}}
to both the local wave vector and the polarisation, and changing the overall sign of the latter.
A direct calculation shows that

R_{\text{BS}}^{(1)}E^{(0)}=\tfrac{1}{2}{m_{\textsc{i}}}h({m_{\textsc{i}}}-{m_{\textsc{ii}}},E^{(0)}+R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})+{m_{\textsc{ii}}}[h({m_{\textsc{ii}}},R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})-h({m_{\textsc{i}}},E^{(0)})]\,,

(7.3)

so that the contribution of this first ray to the output field (omitting the háček) is

\displaystyle F_{\textsc{i}}

\displaystyle={\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\,K_{\textsc{i}}\wedge E_{\textsc{i}}\,e^{i\psi_{\textsc{i}}}\,,

(7.4)

where

\displaystyle\psi_{\textsc{i}}

\displaystyle=2\omega\ell_{\textsc{i}}-\omega t+\varepsilon H({\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x,\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}})+\varepsilon H(k_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{i}})\,,

(7.5)

\displaystyle{\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}-\frac{\varepsilon\omega_{g}\ell_{\textsc{ii}}}{2\sqrt{2}}\left[(n.{m_{\textsc{i}}})\,h^{\prime}({m_{\textsc{i}}},{m_{\textsc{i}}})_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}}}-(1+n.{m_{\textsc{i}}})h^{\prime}({m_{\textsc{i}}},{m_{\textsc{i}}})_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{i}}}\right]+\varepsilon\delta{\mathscr{A}}(x^{E}_{\textsc{i}})\,,

(7.6)

\displaystyle\begin{split}K_{\textsc{i}}&=-\mathrm{d}t-{\nu_{\textsc{ii}}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\left[\omega R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}\kappa+{m_{\textsc{ii}}}(\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}})\right]\left[\frac{h({m_{\textsc{i}}},{m_{\textsc{i}}})}{\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}}}\bigg|_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}}}^{\kappa.x}+\frac{h({m_{\textsc{i}}},{m_{\textsc{i}}})}{\kappa.k_{\textsc{i}}}\bigg|^{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}}}_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{i}}}\right]\,,\end{split}

(7.7)

\displaystyle\begin{split}E_{\textsc{i}}&=R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}\left(\mathbf{1}+\varepsilon\varGamma({\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x,\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}})+\varepsilon R_{\textsc{i}}^{(0)}\varGamma(k_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{i}})\right)E^{(0)}+\varepsilon R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}\delta E^{\parallel}(x^{E}_{\textsc{i}})\\
&-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon{m_{\textsc{i}}}\,h({m_{\textsc{ii}}}-{m_{\textsc{i}}},E^{(0)}+R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x}\\
&+\varepsilon{m_{\textsc{ii}}}\left(2h({m_{\textsc{i}}},E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}}}-h({m_{\textsc{i}}},E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{i}}}-h({m_{\textsc{i}}},E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x}+h({m_{\textsc{ii}}},R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x}\right)\,.\end{split}

(7.8)

Ray II

The second ray is first deflected by the beam splitter, reflects at the mirror surface
{\{{m_{\textsc{ii}}}.x=\ell_{\textsc{ii}}\}}
and is then transmitted by the beam splitter.
In analogy to the first ray, we consider the expression obtained from
Eq.

5.16
by the substitution

\displaystyle m

\displaystyle\to m_{\textsc{ii}}\,,

\displaystyle\ell

\displaystyle\to\ell_{\textsc{ii}}\,,

\displaystyle k

\displaystyle\to k_{\textsc{ii}}\,,

\displaystyle x^{E}

\displaystyle\to x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}}\,,

\displaystyle x^{R}

\displaystyle\to x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}}\,,

(7.9)

where

\displaystyle\mathchoice{(x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-\ell_{\textsc{ii}}\mathchoice{{\check{k}}^{{{\mu}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}}}}{{\check{k}}^{{{\mu}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}}}}{{\check{k}}^{{{\mu}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}}}}{{\check{k}}^{{{\mu}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}}}}/\omega\,,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{(x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{(x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}})^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle=(t-2\ell_{\textsc{ii}},\mathchoice{x^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})\,.

(7.10)

However, due to the deflection at the beam splitter
before
the emission in the detector arm, we must also substitute

\displaystyle E^{(0)}

\displaystyle\to-R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)}\,,

(7.11)

\displaystyle\delta E^{\parallel}

\displaystyle\to-R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}\delta E^{\parallel}+\tfrac{1}{2}{m_{\textsc{i}}}h({m_{\textsc{ii}}}-{m_{\textsc{i}}},E^{(0)}+R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})\,,

(7.12)

as follows from
Eq.

7.3
. This leads to the following expression for the contribution of the second ray to the interferometer output:

\displaystyle F_{\textsc{ii}}

\displaystyle={\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\,K_{\textsc{ii}}\wedge E_{\textsc{ii}}\,e^{i\psi_{\textsc{ii}}}\,,

(7.13)

where

\displaystyle\psi_{\textsc{ii}}

\displaystyle=2\omega\ell_{\textsc{ii}}-\omega t+\pi+\varepsilon H({\check{k}}_{\textsc{ii}},\kappa.x,\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}})+\varepsilon H(k_{\textsc{ii}},\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}},\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}})\,,

(7.14)

\displaystyle{\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}-\frac{\varepsilon\omega_{g}\ell_{\textsc{ii}}}{2\sqrt{2}}\left[(n.{m_{\textsc{ii}}})h^{\prime}({m_{\textsc{ii}}},{m_{\textsc{ii}}})_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}}}-(1+n.{m_{\textsc{ii}}})h^{\prime}({m_{\textsc{ii}}},{m_{\textsc{ii}}})_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}}}\right]+\varepsilon\delta{\mathscr{A}}(x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}})\,,

(7.15)

\displaystyle\begin{split}K_{\textsc{ii}}&=-\mathrm{d}t-{\nu_{\textsc{ii}}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\left[\omega\kappa+{m_{\textsc{ii}}}(\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{ii}})\right]\left[\frac{h({m_{\textsc{ii}}},{m_{\textsc{ii}}})}{\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{ii}}}\bigg|_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}}}^{\kappa.x}+\frac{h({m_{\textsc{ii}}},{m_{\textsc{ii}}})}{\kappa.k_{\textsc{ii}}}\bigg|^{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}}}_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}}}\right]\,,\end{split}

(7.16)

\displaystyle\begin{split}E_{\textsc{ii}}&=\left(\mathbf{1}+\varepsilon\varGamma({\check{k}}_{\textsc{ii}},\kappa.x,\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}})+\varepsilon{R_{\textsc{ii}}}^{(0)}\varGamma(k_{\textsc{ii}},\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}},\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}})\right)R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)}+\varepsilon R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}\delta E^{\parallel}(x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}})\\
&-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon{m_{\textsc{i}}}\,h({m_{\textsc{ii}}}-{m_{\textsc{i}}},E^{(0)}+R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}}}\\
&+\varepsilon{m_{\textsc{ii}}}\left(2h({m_{\textsc{ii}}},R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}}}-h({m_{\textsc{ii}}},R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}}}\right)\,.\end{split}

(7.17)

Here, we have decided to put the
\pi
phase shift at the beam splitter into the eikonal so that all amplitudes are positive.

These results can now be used to compute the final output field.

Interferometer Output

So far, we have worked with a complex electromagnetic field for simplicity. The physical field, however, is obtained by taking the real part. Assuming the emitted polarisation
E
to be real (corresponding to linear polarisation), the real fields are obtained simply by replacing
e^{i\psi}
by
\cos(\psi)
in
Eqs.

7.4
and
7.13
.
The
overall
real field at the output port is thus

{\mathscr{F}}={\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\,K_{\textsc{i}}\wedge E_{\textsc{i}}\,\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}})+{\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\,K_{\textsc{ii}}\wedge E_{\textsc{ii}}\,\cos(\psi_{\textsc{ii}})\,.

(7.18)

There are multiple readout schemes to measure the
gw
perturbations of this resulting field, notably the homodyne, heterodyne, and
dc
readout schemes
[
39
,
40
,
41
]
.
While the homodyne readout scheme was used in the first generation of
gw
detectors, the Advanced
ligo
, Advanced Virgo,
kagra
and
geo 600
detectors now use the
dc
readout scheme (
dcr
)
[
42
,
43
,
44
,
45
]
. However, as the planned
ligo a+
upgrade will transition to the balanced homodyne readout scheme (
bhr
)
[
46
]
, we will describe both the
dcr
and
bhr
schemes in the following analysis.

In all cases, the signal is read out using photo diodes, which are sensitive to the energy density of the
em
field.
As shown in
Appendix

E
, the time-averaged intensity of a field of the form (
7.18
)
at first order in
\varepsilon
is

\braket{\mathchoice{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}}=\tfrac{1}{2}({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\,\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}})^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\,\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}})^{2}+({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}})({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}})\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}}-\psi_{\textsc{ii}})\,,

(7.19)

where, notably, no inner products of the wave vectors and/or polarisation vectors enter. This is because linear perturbations of these normalised vectors produce only quadratic corrections to the relevant inner products and are thus negligible.
This means that although the gravitational wave causes perturbations of the eikonal, amplitude, local wave vector, and the polarisation, only the first three perturbations produce a signal in the detectors considered.

Note that we have chosen the input field to be normalised to unit energy density. In the general case, the expression given here is the output power normalised to the input power.

Equation

7.19
, as well as its generalisation to more than two interfering rays, can be used to compute the signals in the
dcr
and
bhr
schemes. Here and in the following, all expressions are evaluated at the spatial coordinate origin, where the detector is positioned.

DC Readout Scheme

In the
dcr
scheme, one directly measures the intensity of the interferometer output, yielding the signal

S_{\textsc{dcr}}=\tfrac{1}{2}({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\,\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}})^{2}+\tfrac{1}{2}({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\,\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}})^{2}+({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}})({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}})\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}}-\psi_{\textsc{ii}})\,.

(7.20)

In the unperturbed case, this reduces to

S_{\textsc{dcr}}^{(0)}=\tfrac{1}{2}-\tfrac{1}{2}\cos(2\omega(\ell_{\textsc{i}}-\ell_{\textsc{ii}}))=\sin^{2}(\omega(\ell_{\textsc{i}}-\ell_{\textsc{ii}}))\,,

(7.21)

which vanishes if both arm lengths are chosen to be equal.
The gravitational wave produces three correction terms, corresponding to perturbations of the eikonal, amplitude, and wave vector:

S_{\textsc{dcr}}=S_{\textsc{dcr}}^{(0)}+\varepsilon S_{{\textsc{dcr}},\psi}+\varepsilon S_{{\textsc{dcr}},{\mathscr{A}}}+\varepsilon S_{{\textsc{dcr}},K}+O(\varepsilon^{2})\,.

(7.22)

Using
Eqs.

7.5
—
7.7
,
Eqs.

7.14
—
7.16
, as well as
Eq.

4.11
, these first-order perturbations evaluate to

\displaystyle\begin{split}S_{{\textsc{dcr}},\psi}={}&+\tfrac{1}{2}\sin(2\omega(\ell_{\textsc{i}}-\ell_{\textsc{ii}}))\\
&\times\bigg(H({\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x,\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}})+H(k_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{i}})\\
&\qquad-H({\check{k}}_{\textsc{ii}},\kappa.x,\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}})-H(k_{\textsc{ii}},\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}},\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}})\bigg)\,,\end{split}

(7.23)

\displaystyle\begin{split}S_{{\textsc{dcr}},{\mathscr{A}}}={}&-\tfrac{1}{2}\sin^{2}(\omega(\ell_{\textsc{i}}-\ell_{\textsc{ii}}))\\
&\times\bigg(\omega\ell_{\textsc{i}}\left[(n.{m_{\textsc{i}}})\,h^{\prime}({m_{\textsc{i}}},{m_{\textsc{i}}})_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}}}-(1+n.{m_{\textsc{i}}})h^{\prime}({m_{\textsc{i}}},{m_{\textsc{i}}})_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{i}}}\right]\\
&\qquad+\omega\ell_{\textsc{ii}}\left[(n.{m_{\textsc{ii}}})\,h^{\prime}({m_{\textsc{ii}}},{m_{\textsc{ii}}})_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}}}-(1+n.{m_{\textsc{ii}}})h^{\prime}({m_{\textsc{ii}}},{m_{\textsc{ii}}})_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}}}\right]\bigg)\,,\end{split}

(7.24)

\displaystyle\begin{split}S_{{\textsc{dcr}},K}={}&-\tfrac{1}{2}\omega_{g}\omega\sin^{2}(\omega(\ell_{\textsc{i}}-\ell_{\textsc{ii}}))\\
&\times\bigg(\frac{h({m_{\textsc{i}}},{m_{\textsc{i}}})}{\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}}}\bigg|_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}}}^{\kappa.x}+\frac{h({m_{\textsc{i}}},{m_{\textsc{i}}})}{\kappa.k_{\textsc{i}}}\bigg|^{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}}}_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{i}}}+\frac{h({m_{\textsc{ii}}},{m_{\textsc{ii}}})}{\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{ii}}}\bigg|_{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}}}^{\kappa.x}+\frac{h({m_{\textsc{ii}}},{m_{\textsc{ii}}})}{\kappa.k_{\textsc{ii}}}\bigg|^{\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{ii}}}_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}}}\bigg)\,,\end{split}

(7.25)

all of which vanish for equal arm lengths. Hence, the
dcr
scheme requires a slight asymmetry
\Delta\ell=\ell_{\textsc{i}}-\ell_{\textsc{ii}}
to obtain a linear response to the
gw
perturbation.

The three terms appearing here can be interpreted as follows.

S_{{\textsc{dcr}},\psi}
arises due to the perturbation of the optical path lengths along the two interferometer arms. The multiplicative factor
{\sin(2\omega(\ell_{\textsc{i}}-\ell_{\textsc{ii}}))}
entails that the phase response vanishes when the unperturbed output power is extremal, and is maximal in between.
The second term,
S_{{\textsc{dcr}},{\mathscr{A}}}
, arises from amplitude perturbations, which can be regarded as scintillations of the light signal, which grow with the length of the interferometer arms (for very long distances, corrections from higher order terms of the geometrical optics expansion might become relevant). The strength of this effect is directly proportional to the output intensity in the unperturbed configuration, as can be seen from the multiplicative factor
\sin^{2}(\omega(\ell_{\textsc{i}}-\ell_{\textsc{ii}}))
. Finally, the last term,
S_{{\textsc{dcr}},K}
, arises because the gravitational wave modulates the frequency of the light rays, which translates to a modulation of the output power, similar to the amplitude modulation.

Hence, the first contribution,
S_{{\textsc{dcr}},\psi}
, describes the standard phase response (as it is determined from the eikonal alone), while the remaining terms describe the corrections beyond the eikonal equation.

Balanced Homodyne Readout Scheme

In the
bhr
scheme, one mixes the output field (
7.18
) with a further ray — typically derived from the same laser source — which is not sent through the interferometer.
For this ray the
gw
perturbations are negligible so that one may set

\displaystyle\psi_{\textsc{bhr}}

\displaystyle=-\omega t+\varphi

\displaystyle{\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{bhr}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\,,

\displaystyle K_{\textsc{bhr}}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{d}t+\nu_{\textsc{i}}\,,

(7.26)

where
\varphi
denotes the readout angle (an experimentally controlled parameter), and the amplitude normalisation is the same as in
Eq.

6.5
.
Sending this ray and the output field (
7.18
) orthogonally through a further 50:50 beam splitter, the resulting intensities at the two output ports evaluate to

\displaystyle\begin{split}I={}&\tfrac{1}{4}S_{\textsc{dcr}}+\tfrac{1}{8}({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{bhr}}\,\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.36363pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.25972pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{bhr}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.36363pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.25972pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{bhr}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.36363pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.25972pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{bhr}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.36363pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.25972pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{bhr}}}{0}}}})^{2}\\
&+\tfrac{1}{4}({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{bhr}}\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.36363pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.25972pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{bhr}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.36363pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.25972pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{bhr}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.36363pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.25972pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{bhr}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.95839pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.36363pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.25972pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{bhr}}}{0}}}})\left[({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\mathchoice{K^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{K^{{\m
