akebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}\mathchoice{\mathbf{E}^{{{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathbf{E}^{{{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathbf{E}^{{{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathbf{E}^{{{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,,

(C.10)

where
\tilde{\delta}
is the divergence operator of the intersection of
\Sigma
with a time-slice, and similarly denoting the exterior derivative intrinsic to such a surface by
\tilde{\mathrm{d}}
, one obtains the constraint equations in the form

\displaystyle\tilde{\delta}\mathbf{E}+a_{\parallel}\cdot\mathbf{E}

\displaystyle=0\,,

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathrm{d}}\mathbf{E}+a_{\parallel}\wedge\mathbf{E}

\displaystyle=0\,,

(C.11)

where
a_{\parallel}=a-n(n\cdot a)
is the tangential part of the acceleration, and where we have identified the vector
a
with its metric-equivalent one-form.
In particular, for geodesic
u
, this simplifies to

\displaystyle\tilde{\delta}\mathbf{E}

\displaystyle=0\,,

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathrm{d}}\mathbf{E}

\displaystyle=0\,,

(C.12)

which entails that
\mathbf{E}
satisfies
\Delta_{\text{HdR}}\mathbf{E}=0
, where
\Delta_{\text{HdR}}
is the Hodge-de Rham Laplacian. Moreover, on a contractible region the second equation implies that
\mathbf{E}
is exact so that the problem reduces to the scalar Laplace equation

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\chi\,,

where

\displaystyle\Delta\chi

\displaystyle=0\,.

(C.13)

Here,
\Delta
denotes the spatial scalar Laplacian on
\Sigma
. Note that
\chi
is not required to remain bounded: instead, since the physically relevant field is
\mathbf{E}=\mathrm{d}\chi
, we require
\chi
to have bounded first derivatives.

To construct perturbative solutions, it is convenient to write the Laplace equation using the “densitised contravariant metric” in the form

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{A}}}}(\mathchoice{\tilde{\mathfrak{g}}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{\mathfrak{g}}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{\mathfrak{g}}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{\mathfrak{g}}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{B}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{B}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{B}}}}{\partial^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{B}}}}\chi)=0\,,

(C.14)

where
\mathchoice{\tilde{\mathfrak{g}}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{\mathfrak{g}}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{\mathfrak{g}}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{\mathfrak{g}}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\sqrt{\det\tilde{g}}\,\mathchoice{\tilde{g}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{g}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{g}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{g}^{{{A}{B}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
, with
\tilde{g}
being the metric induced by
g
on
\Sigma
(at any instant of time). Inserting the perturbative expansion

\chi=\mathchoice{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}\mathchoice{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{A}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\varepsilon\chi^{(1)}\,,

(C.15)

one obtains

(C.18)

and choosing the constants

E^{(1)}
assumes a prescribed value at the spatial origin, one obtains

\mathchoice{E^{{{{(1)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}{E^{{{{(1)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}{E^{{{{(1)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}{E^{{{{(1)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}=\mathchoice{E^{{{{(1)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{A}}}}{E^{{{{(1)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675 (C.19)

Appendix D
Boundary Conditions for Perfect Reflection

In this section, we give a generally covariant description of perfect reflection at the level of geometrical optics.
For this, we consider a field of the form

\displaystyle F

\displaystyle=F_{1}+F_{2}\,,

\displaystyle F_{1,2}

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{d}\psi_{1,2})\wedge E_{1,2}\,e^{i\omega\psi_{1,2}}\,,

(D.1)

where
F_{1}
describes a wave impinging on a reflecting surface
\Sigma
, and
F_{2}
is the reflected wave.
We first consider how
\mathrm{d}\psi_{2}
relates to
\mathrm{d}\psi_{1}
(on
\Sigma
), and then we determine
E_{2}
from
E_{1}
.

For the eikonal functions, we assume the standard matching condition that
\psi_{1}
and
\psi_{2}
coincide on
\Sigma
, cf. e.g. Ref.
[
36
, Sect. 2.5]
.
This entails that
\mathrm{d}\psi_{2}=\mathrm{d}\psi_{1}+\alpha\nu
(on
\Sigma
), where
\nu
is the unit conormal to
\Sigma
, and
\alpha
is some function on
\Sigma
which can be determined as follows. Since we are concerned with waves propagating in different directions, we must have
\alpha\neq 0
. Further, taking the norm on both sides and using the fact that both functions
\psi_{1,2}
satisfy the eikonal equation, one obtains
\alpha=-2\mathchoice{\nu^{{{\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\nu^{{{\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\nu^{{{\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\nu^{{{\sigma}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}\psi_{1}
. Using the definition (
5.3
), this can be written as

\mathchoice{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\rho}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\rho}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\rho}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\rho}}}}\psi_{2}=\mathchoice{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}\psi_{1}\,,

(D.2)

i.e. the wave vector is simply reflected along the normal to
\Sigma
.

We now turn to the relationship between
E_{1}
and
E_{2}
.
For this, consider an arbitrary point on
\Sigma
(which we shall keep fixed from now on) and let
u
denote any (future-pointing) unit timelike vector at this point, which is orthogonal to
\nu
(the final result will be independent of this choice). By the argument of
Appendix

B
, we may assume without loss of generality that the covectors
E_{1,2}
are orthogonal to
u
.
Using local inertial coordinates adapted to
u
(at the considered point), we may write

\mathrm{d}\psi_{1,2}=\frac{\omega_{\mathscr{O}}}{\omega}(m_{1,2}-\mathrm{d}t)\,,

(D.3)

where
\omega_{\mathscr{O}}
is the frequency measured by observers with four-velocity
u
, cf. (
B.2
), and where
m_{1,2}
are spatial unit covectors which are related by

\mathchoice{m^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}{m^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}{m^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}{m^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}=\mathchoice{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{m^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}{\sigma}}}}{m^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}{\sigma}}}}{m^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}{\sigma}}}}{m^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}{\sigma}}}}
.
On
\Sigma
, the field strength then evaluates to

F=\left[-\mathrm{d}t\wedge(E_{1}+E_{2})+m_{1}\wedge E_{1}+m_{2}\wedge E_{2}\right]\tfrac{\omega_{\mathscr{O}}}{\omega}e^{i\omega\psi}\,,

(D.4)

and comparison with
Eq.

B.3
yields

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}

\displaystyle=(E_{1}+E_{2})\tfrac{\omega_{\mathscr{O}}}{\omega}e^{i\omega\psi}\,,

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}

\displaystyle=(m_{1}\wedge E_{1}+m_{2}\wedge E_{2})\tfrac{\omega_{\mathscr{O}}}{\omega}e^{i\omega\psi}\,.

(D.5)

Now, since we are working in a local inertial frame, we may use the standard equations

\displaystyle\vec{\nu}\times\vec{\mathbf{E}}

\displaystyle=0\,,

\displaystyle\vec{\nu}\cdot\vec{\mathbf{B}}

\displaystyle=0\,,

(D.6)

which are equivalent to the following equations, in exterior algebra notation:

\displaystyle\nu\wedge\mathbf{E}

\displaystyle=0\,,

\displaystyle\nu\wedge\mathbf{B}

\displaystyle=0\,.

(D.7)

The first equation here entails that
E_{2}=-E_{1}+\beta\nu
for some constant
\beta
, and the condition of perfect reflection requires that
E_{1}
and
E_{2}
have the same norm, yielding

\mathchoice{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}=-\mathchoice{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}{\sigma}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}{\sigma}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}{\sigma}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{1}{\sigma}}}}\,.

(D.8)

The second matching condition
\nu\wedge\mathbf{B}=0
is then automatically satisfied. Indeed, since
{\nu\wedge m_{2}=\nu\wedge m_{1}}
, one has

\nu\wedge\mathbf{B}=\nu\wedge m_{1}\wedge\mathbf{E}\,,

(D.9)

which vanishes because
\nu\wedge\mathbf{E}=0
.

So far, we have shown that in local inertial coordinates (using a decomposition of
F
adapted to the four-velocity
u
) the conditions for perfect reflection are

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\rho}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\rho}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\rho}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\rho}}}}\psi_{2}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{R^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\sigma}}}_{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.63394pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.79993pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.99994pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\sigma}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.63394pt][c] \displaystyle\mathchoice{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\rho}}}}

(D.10)

which, together, imply

\mathchoice{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{2}{\alpha}{\beta}}}}{F^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$ (D.11)

But since this last equation (which is independent of the decomposition of
F
into
\mathbf{E}
and
\mathbf{B}
) is tensorial, it holds in all coordinate systems, and since the considered point on
\Sigma
was arbitrary, it holds everywhere on this surface.

Appendix E
Output Power of an Interferometer

In
Eq.

7.18
, the field at the output port of the interferometer was found to be of the form

{\mathscr{F}}={\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\,k_{\textsc{i}}\wedge E_{\textsc{i}}\,\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}})+{\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\,k_{\textsc{ii}}\wedge E_{\textsc{ii}}\,\cos(\psi_{\textsc{ii}})\,,

(E.1)

where
k_{\textsc{i}}
and
k_{\textsc{ii}}
are null vectors, and
E_{\textsc{i}}
and
E_{\textsc{ii}}
are spacelike unit vectors (with respect to the full space-time metric
g
), which satisfy
g(k_{\textsc{i}},E_{\textsc{i}})=0
and
g(k_{\textsc{ii}},E_{\textsc{ii}})=0
.
In this section, we compute the time-averaged energy density for such a field at first order in the
gw
amplitude
\varepsilon
.

The intensity measured by a photodetector is given by the temporal component of the energy-momentum tensor

\mathchoice{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}
are of order
\varepsilon
as well, so that both summands in the last line are of second order and thus negligible.

Hence, to first order in
\varepsilon
one has

\begin{split}\mathchoice{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{\alpha}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{\alpha}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{\alpha}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{\alpha}}}}\mathchoice{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{\beta}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\di (E.6)

Now consider the second term in the energy-momentum tensor:

\mathchoice{{\mathscr{F}}^{{{\alpha}{\beta}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{{\alpha}{\beta}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{{\alpha}{\beta}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{{\alpha}{\beta}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathchoice{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}}}}{{\mathscr{F}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}}}}=4{\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\,{\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\,\left[g(k_{\textsc{i}},k_{\textsc{ii}})g(E_{\textsc{i}},E_{\textsc{ii}})-g(k_{\textsc{i}},E_{\textsc{ii}})g(E_{\textsc{i}},k_{\textsc{ii}})\right]\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}})\cos(\psi_{\textsc{ii}})\,.

(E.7)

By a similar argument as before, one finds this expression to be of order
\varepsilon^{2}
. Hence, at first order in
\varepsilon
, the energy density reduces to

\mathchoice{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}=\left[{\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\,\mathchoice{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}})+{\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\,\mathchoice{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}\cos(\psi_{\textsc{ii}})\right]^{2}\,.

(E.8)

Finally, we compute the time average of this expression since the read-out cannot resolve the fast oscillations of the electromagnetic field itself but only the slow modulation arising from the gravitational wave.
(Recall that optical frequencies are in the order of
500\,\text{THz}
while observed
gw
frequencies are roughly in the range of
10\;\text{to}\;500\,\text{Hz}
).
Formally, such an average is given by

\displaystyle\braket{f(t)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\int_{t}^{t+T}f(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,,

\displaystyle\text{where }\omega\gg T^{-1}\gg\omega_{g}\,.

(E.9)

In particular, the amplitudes
{\mathscr{A}}
, the polarisation vectors
E
and the wave vectors
k
oscillate slowly and may be taken outside the average, leaving us only with the eikonal terms, which oscillate rapidly in time as
-\omega t
. Consequently, we have

\displaystyle\braket{\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}})^{2}}=\braket{\cos(\psi_{\textsc{ii}})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\,,

\displaystyle\braket{\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}})\cos(\psi_{\textsc{ii}})}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}}-\psi_{\textsc{ii}})\,,

(E.10)

so that the averaged energy density at the output is

\braket{\mathchoice{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}{T^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{0}{0}}}}}=\tfrac{1}{2}({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{i}}\,\mathchoice{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[0.97221pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.2639pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.36111pt][c]{ }{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{i}}}{0}}}})({\mathscr{A}}_{\textsc{ii}}\,\mathchoice{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.5278pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.94443pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{{\textsc{ii}}}{0}}}})\cos(\psi_{\textsc{i}}-\psi_{\textsc{ii}})\,.

(E.11)

To conclude this section, we verify explicitly that the conditions (
E.5
) are satisfied for the fields given in the main body of the text.
Writing
Eqs.

7.7
and
7.16
as

\displaystyle K_{\textsc{i}}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{d}t+\nu_{\textsc{ii}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon\alpha[\omega R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}\kappa+{m_{\textsc{ii}}}(\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}}))]\,,

(E.12)

\displaystyle K_{\textsc{ii}}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{d}t+\nu_{\textsc{ii}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon\beta[\omega\kappa+{m_{\textsc{ii}}}(\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{ii}}))]\,,

(E.13)

where the precise form of the factors
\alpha
and
\beta
is irrelevant for the argument here, one finds

g(K_{\textsc{i}},K_{\textsc{ii}})=\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon\alpha\left[\omega(-\mathrm{d}t-{m_{\textsc{i}}}).\kappa+\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}}\right]+\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon\beta\left[\omega(-\mathrm{d}t-{m_{\textsc{ii}}}).\kappa+\kappa.{\check{k}}_{\textsc{ii}}\right]+O(\varepsilon^{2})\,,

(E.14)

which vanishes since
{\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}}=\omega(-\mathrm{d}t-{m_{\textsc{i}}})
and
{\check{k}}_{\textsc{ii}}=\omega(-\mathrm{d}t-{m_{\textsc{ii}}})
.

Similarly, using the expressions for the polarisation vectors from
Eqs.

7.8
and
7.17
together with the explicit form of
\delta E^{\parallel}
given in
Eq.

4.10
, as well the fact that
R_{\textsc{i}}^{(0)}
,
R_{\textsc{ii}}^{(0)}
and
R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}
are orthogonal matrices, their inner product evaluates to

\begin{split}g(E_{\textsc{i}},E_{\textsc{ii}})={}&1+\varepsilon\mathchoice{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}[\mathchoice{\varGamma({\check{k}}_{\textsc{i}},\kappa.x,\kappa.x^{R}_{\textsc{i}})^{{{\mu}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2. &-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon h(E^{(0)},E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x}+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon h(E^{(0)},E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{i}}}\\
&-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon h(R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)},R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x}+\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon h(R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)},R_{\text{BS}}^{(0)}E^{(0)})_{\kappa.x^{E}_{\textsc{ii}}}+O(\varepsilon^{2})\,.\end{split}

(E.15)

This reduces to
g(E_{\textsc{i}},E_{\textsc{ii}})=1+O(\varepsilon^{2})
by virtue of the explicit form of the integrated Christoffel symbols given in
Eq.

4.32
.
